High Izbo! In accordance with the provisions of the Article. 12th Ombudsperson of the Child Rights Act I present to the High House information on the Ombudsman's activities for 2017 and comments on the state of compliance with the rights of the child. I stand before the High House for the tenth and last time, because at the end of August my second and last term of office of the Ombudsman of the rights of the child passes. Decade of changes in the perception and protection of the child, childhood, rights of the child, subjectivity of the child, child's citizenship. The changes that are behind today are not only the spokesperson for the rights of the child himself, but a large group of familiar names and unknown people of various professions, with the highest scientific titles and best experience in social, professional and public work for children. During this time, a number of important changes took place at the initiative or with significant involvement of the Ombudsman in Polish legislation, including. strengthening the various forms of support for children and their families, strengthening the protection of the rights of children deprived of their ability to raise in the biological family, strengthening the protection of the rights of sick and hospitalized children, including the restoration of the medical document: the children's health book and the creation of a system for monitoring the fate of the child from birth, and the placing and leaving of a pediatrician in the primary care system, introducing a statutory explicit ban on the use of physical penalties against children, strengthening the protection of children against all forms of sexual abuse, which allowed Poland to ratify so-called. the Lanzarote Convention, the introduction of the concept of child's right to both parents, the strengthening of legal protection for child crimes, including the establishment of a child's priority, increased penalties for child harm offences, the introduction of a legal obligation for natural persons to notify law enforcement authorities of their knowledge of child harm, the strengthening of the protection of children's rights in nursing, educational, medical and socio-therapeutic institutions, the strengthening of the subjective treatment of children by individuals and institutions making decisions on matters relating to the child, the development of legal instruments protecting children's rights in an international colour conflict. In fulfilling the request made by the Polish Ombudsman, the United Nations General Assembly adopted the Third Protocol to the Convention on the Rights of the Child, enabling the Committee of the Rights of the Child to adopt and deal with individual complaints against national infringements of the rights of the child. Today, consistently as a spokesperson for the rights of the child, I again call on the Polish Government and Parliament to ratify this document. [[Applause]] This expected decision of the Polish authorities is not a gesture, but an obligation which falls within the area of responsibility for children. A detailed description of the general and individual actions undertaken in 2017 is provided in the information presented to the High House. Therefore, here I will summarize and compare only statistical data for 2017 and the whole decade. A total of 39 182 cases were brought to the Ombudsman in 2017 and for 10 years of office - 331 381 cases. The Ombudsman addressed a total of 168 general speeches to ministers, bodies or institutions in 2017 and 1148 general speeches for 10 years, proposing amendments to specific provisions. In 2017, in 363 cases, the Ombudsman considered that there was a risk to the interests of the child in the ongoing judicial proceedings, so that he had taken part in court proceedings and participated in them on the rights of the prosecutor. Within 10 years of such accessions and continuation of accessions, there were 2104. An important element of the Ombudsman's activities was the taking of control measures. In 2017, the Ombudsman examined 291 cases at the place of the child's rights, and during the last decade of these checks there was a total of 1609. As part of the dissemination of the rights of the child last year, the Ombudsman met with 52 000. children and young people in Poland. During both my term of office, more than 487 thousand attended such meetings. Polish children. The honorary patronage of the Ombudsman was covered by 299 events in 2017. The sum of the decade is 1842 awarded honorary patronages of the Ombudsman. There are four important events that are the result of very good cooperation with the Sejm. I would like to express my appreciation for the right initiatives of the spokesman and the adoption of the year 2012 by Janusz Korczak, 2018 - the Year of Irena Sendler, the National Day of Preschool and the Polish Children's Rights Day. The information presented to the High House in accordance with the provisions of the Act covers five main areas of action for the protection of the rights of the youngest citizens of the Republic of Poland. These include the right to life and health, the right to family education, the right to decent social conditions, the right to science and the right to protection against violence, cruelty, exploitation, demoralisation, neglect and other mistreatment. Janusz Korczak said to us in his will: "You must not leave the world as it is. In response to this challenge, after more than six years of very hard, social work set up by the Ombudsman of the Children's Rights Commission for the Codification of Family Law under the direction of Professor Leszek Stadniczek at the end of my second term, I will present to the President, Parliament and the Government a proposal for a comprehensive change of Polish family law, which aims to further strengthen the position of the child and the family. [[Applause]] Today, I would like to present only a fragment of the material developed, which was created through many reflections, analyses and compromises. The draft Family Code opens a preamble, which I will quote as it reflects the nature of the project as a whole: In respect of human dignity, the Republic of Poland protects the welfare of the child and the welfare of the family, supports the family, including marriage, and parenthood, in accordance with the principle of subsidiarity creates conditions for the proper functioning of the family. Given the exceptional role of family law in the protection of the rights and freedoms of the child, family rights and its multifacetedness in the process of law-making and application: - having regard to the generally recognised values listed in the Constitution of the Republic of Poland and the existing acts of international law, in particular the natural dignity of man as a source of his rights; - acting for the benefit of the child and providing him with decent living conditions in the family, which is his natural environment and to satisfy his physical, mental, social and moral needs, shaping his personality; - having regard to the need to provide the child with an educational environment which guarantees him a comprehensive development; - recognising the family as a basic community of persons and a social institution; - with a view to strengthening the position and function of the family; - preferring dialogue and mediation as tools for resolving conflicts and family disputes; - having regard to the particular role of the family judiciary in protecting the values indicated and the need to implement and develop its ideas; - while respecting the axiological unity of the law and the resulting need for the coherence of the family's substantive, procedural and implementing law, the following is hereby established (...). In the new code, as the spokesperson for the rights of the child, I declare the need to move away from the pejorative and conflictogenic foreign concept of the concept of parental authority for parental responsibility, the proposed definition of which is: [[Applause]] Parental responsibility is the task, attitude and relationship of parents with the child, carried out with respect for his dignity and rights, in accordance with the socio-legal order. It is aimed at caring for the child and satisfying his needs, preparing for family and society life and fulfilling the maintenance obligation. It shall be implemented in particular by implementation [[♪ The bell ♪]] responsibilities and rights in relation to the care of the person and the property of the child, representation, personal relations, the origin of the child, his name and place of residence. It applies mutatis mutandis to persons who, by judgment of the court, are liable to the child as much as their parents. Power, power, man's control over another man, absolute obedience are concepts close to totalitarianism, objectification of man, enslavement, possession. They are not conducive to proper parent-child and child-parent relationships. In this document, as a spokesman for the rights of the child, I propose for the first time to introduce into Polish law the definition of the good of the child as a state in which the child achieves correct, comprehensive and harmonious mental, physical and social development, respecting his dignity and the resulting natural rights. This good is shaped in particular by positive family, personal and educational relationships. The draft code treats a child, in accordance with the Act on the Prosecutor of the Rights of the Child, as any human being from conception to adulthood. An important part of the opening of the new family code is the Family Law Rules, which I cannot even mention today due to the limited speaking time.

Children's Phone of Trust and Interested Party Team. Children love this phone and use it very often. Education and Education, whose tasks include: monitoring the respect of the rights of the child and intervening in matters relating to the respect of the right to education, care and education, access to cultural goods, entertainment, leisure, including in matters relating to sports, artistic activities, development of talents and interests. Many times the spokesperson of children's rights in such artistic events, sports participates personally, making these children very fun. Children like to be with him and like to listen to what he has to say to them. Another team is the International and Constitutional Affairs Team, whose tasks include:: study of Polish law in terms of its compliance with international obligations, in particular with the Convention on the Rights of the Child and the Conventions and Recommendations adopted within the Council of Europe, drawing up opinions on draft legal acts regarding their compliance with the aforementioned international law documents, analysing new instruments of international law, analysing government reports for international control bodies, drawing up opinions and proposals on the compatibility of laws with the Polish Constitution and international agreements and providing assistance to Polish children residing abroad and foreign children residing in the territory of the Republic of Poland. Another team is the Family and Minor Affairs Team, whose tasks include: monitoring the respect of the rights of the child and intervening in matters relating to the exercise and observance of the right to family education, residence in a foster family or a 24-hour care and protection of the child against violence, cruelty, exploitation, demoralisation, negligence and other ill-treatment. Another team is the Social Affairs and Administrative Law Group, which includes tasks. monitor the respect of the rights of the child and intervene in matters relating to the respect of the right to decent social conditions and the right to life and health. There is also the Office of the Ombudsman of Children's Rights, whose tasks include the substantive and organisational support for the tasks carried out by the Ombudsman of children's rights, as well as the Administrative Team, whose tasks include the organisational and administrative and financial service of the Office of the Ombudsman of Children's Rights, as well as many thematic teams established over the years. The Ombudsperson of Children's Rights Act gave him powers of warning, signalling and initiative and gave him competence to present to the competent public authorities, organisations and institutes assessments and conclusions aimed at effectively protecting the rights and interests of the child. The Ombudsman may also request the competent authorities to take a legislative initiative or to issue or amend other acts. Today we are referring to the entire activity of the Ombudsman of the rights of the child. The administrative court dismissed the action by the voivode, declaring it unreasonable, stating that Konrad Gęsierz did not violate the prohibition to combine the mandate of the municipal councillor in Namysłów with the function of the deputy minister of Namysłowski. Therefore, the resolution of the county council on the election of Konrad Gęsieraz was correct and the mandate of the city councilman expired by law at the time of the election as vice-stars. The legal situation seems obvious. It is appalling that after 8 years of office, when his services included the legal supervision of the resolutions of various local governments, Mr Ryszard Wilczyński did not realise that the election of the vice-stars of Namysłowski took place in accordance with the law, and used this speech in the Polish parliament to conduct local politics, to mislead the public and voters, the inhabitants of Namysłow.
